#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;

// Index calculation for flattened 2D array
inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
    return j * n + i;
}

void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes, 
                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    unsigned int seed = 42;
    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);
    }
    
    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    }
}

void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {
    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            path[idx2(i, j, numNodes)] = j;
            path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = i;
        }
        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
    }
}

void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
                   const size_t numNodes) {
    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
    // For each intermediate node k
    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
        // For each source node i
        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
            // For each destination node j
            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;
                
                if (newDist < distIJ) {
                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {
    // Basic sanity checks
    
    // 1. Diagonal should be zero
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {
            printf("Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\n", i, i);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]
    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time
    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];
                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];
                
                // Check for overflow before addition
                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {
                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {
                        printf("Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\n", 
                               i, j, k);
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numNodes = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);
    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing graph...\n");
    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);
    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);
    
    // Run Floyd-Warshall
    printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate operations per second
    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity
    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;
    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)
    if (printResults) {
        print_results_int(dist, "DistanceMatrix");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

 #include <algorithm>
+#include <cerrno>
 #include <chrono>
 #include <cmath>
 #include <cstdio>

 #include <vector>

 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
 
+namespace {
+
+constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int THREAD_ROWS = 8;
+constexpr int ROWS_PER_THREAD = TILE_SIZE / THREAD_ROWS;
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+struct Tile {
+    unsigned int distance[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    unsigned int witness[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+};
+
+// A witness is one plus the largest internal vertex on a shortest path; zero
+// denotes an unchanged direct edge. Among equal distances prefer the smallest
+// witness. This preserves the original ascending-k, strict-improvement path
+// matrix even though the blocked algorithm visits candidates in another order.
+__device__ __forceinline__ void relax(unsigned int& distance,
+                                      unsigned int& witness,
+                                      unsigned int leftDistance,
+                                      unsigned int rightDistance,
+                                      unsigned int leftWitness,
+                                      unsigned int rightWitness,
+                                      unsigned int intermediate) {
+    const unsigned int candidate = leftDistance + rightDistance;
+    if (candidate <= distance) {
+        const unsigned int candidateWitness =
+            max(max(leftWitness, rightWitness), intermediate);
+        if (candidate < distance || candidateWitness < witness) {
+            distance = candidate;
+            witness = candidateWitness;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ void loadTile(Tile& tile,
+                                         const unsigned int* distance,
+                                         const unsigned int* witness,
+                                         int tileRow, int tileColumn,
+                                         size_t stride) {
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        tile.distance[row][column] = distance[index];
+        tile.witness[row][column] = witness[index];
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ void storeTile(const Tile& tile,
+                                          unsigned int* distance,
+                                          unsigned int* witness,
+                                          int tileRow, int tileColumn,
+                                          size_t stride) {
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        distance[index] = tile.distance[row][column];
+        witness[index] = tile.witness[row][column];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void initializeDeviceMatrix(unsigned int* distance,
+                                        unsigned int* witness,
+                                        size_t stride, size_t numNodes) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        witness[index] = 0;
+        const size_t row = index / stride;
+        const size_t column = index % stride;
+        if (row >= numNodes || column >= numNodes) {
+            distance[index] = row == column ? 0 : INF;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Phase 1: close the diagonal tile. Pivot rows/columns are read-only during
+// each iteration (zero diagonal), so one barrier per k is sufficient.
+__global__ void closePivot(unsigned int* distance, unsigned int* witness,
+                           size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile tile;
+    loadTile(tile, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+    __syncthreads();
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            if (row != k && column != k) {
+                relax(tile.distance[row][column], tile.witness[row][column],
+                      tile.distance[row][k], tile.distance[k][column],
+                      tile.witness[row][k], tile.witness[k][column],
+                      pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    storeTile(tile, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+}
+
+// Phase 2: all tiles in the pivot row and column run independently.
+template <bool PivotRow>
+__global__ void closePanel(unsigned int* distance, unsigned int* witness,
+                           size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile diagonal;
+    __shared__ Tile panel;
+    const int other = int(blockIdx.x) + (int(blockIdx.x) >= pivot);
+    const int tileRow = PivotRow ? pivot : other;
+    const int tileColumn = PivotRow ? other : pivot;
+    loadTile(diagonal, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+    loadTile(panel, distance, witness, tileRow, tileColumn, stride);
+    __syncthreads();
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            if constexpr (PivotRow) {
+                if (row != k) {
+                    relax(panel.distance[row][column], panel.witness[row][column],
+                          diagonal.distance[row][k], panel.distance[k][column],
+                          diagonal.witness[row][k], panel.witness[k][column],
+                          pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+                }
+            } else {
+                if (column != k) {
+                    relax(panel.distance[row][column], panel.witness[row][column],
+                          panel.distance[row][k], diagonal.distance[k][column],
+                          panel.witness[row][k], diagonal.witness[k][column],
+                          pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    storeTile(panel, distance, witness, tileRow, tileColumn, stride);
+}
+
+// Phase 3 dominates the work: load each panel once, retain four output cells
+// per thread in registers, and reuse the panels without inner-loop barriers.
+__global__ void updateRemaining(unsigned int* __restrict__ distance,
+                                unsigned int* __restrict__ witness,
+                                size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile left;
+    __shared__ Tile right;
+    const int tileRow = int(blockIdx.y) + (int(blockIdx.y) >= pivot);
+    const int tileColumn = int(blockIdx.x) + (int(blockIdx.x) >= pivot);
+    loadTile(left, distance, witness, tileRow, pivot, stride);
+    loadTile(right, distance, witness, pivot, tileColumn, stride);
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    unsigned int outputDistance[ROWS_PER_THREAD];
+    unsigned int outputWitness[ROWS_PER_THREAD];
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        outputDistance[r] = distance[index];
+        outputWitness[r] = witness[index];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int rightDistance = right.distance[k][column];
+        const unsigned int rightWitness = right.witness[k][column];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            relax(outputDistance[r], outputWitness[r],
+                  left.distance[row][k], rightDistance,
+                  left.witness[row][k], rightWitness,
+                  pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        distance[index] = outputDistance[r];
+        witness[index] = outputWitness[r];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void decodePaths(unsigned int* witness, size_t stride) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const unsigned int value = witness[index];
+        // initializePathMatrix initializes each entry to its source row.
+        witness[index] = value ? value - 1 : unsigned(index / stride);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
 // Index calculation for flattened 2D array
 inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;
@@ -44,26 +256,62 @@

+    initializeDeviceMatrix<<<linearBlocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(
+        deviceDistance, deviceWitness, stride, numNodes);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "initializing GPU padding and witnesses");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(deviceDistance, stride * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                               dist.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                               numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                               cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
+              "copying distances to GPU");
+
+    const dim3 threads(TILE_SIZE, THREAD_ROWS);
+    for (int pivot = 0; pivot < tiles; ++pivot) {
+        closePivot<<<1, threads, 0, stream>>>(deviceDistance, deviceWitness,
+                                             stride, pivot);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching diagonal tile");
+        if (tiles > 1) {
+            closePanel<true><<<tiles - 1, threads, 0, stream>>>(
+                deviceDistance, deviceWitness, stride, pivot);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching pivot row");
+            closePanel<false><<<tiles - 1, threads, 0, stream>>>(
+                deviceDistance, deviceWitness, stride, pivot);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching pivot column");
+            updateRemaining<<<dim3(tiles - 1, tiles - 1), threads, 0, stream>>>(
+                deviceDistance, deviceWitness, stride, pivot);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching remaining tiles");
         }
     }
+    decodePaths<<<linearBlocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(deviceWitness, stride);
+    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "decoding path witnesses");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(dist.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                               deviceDistance, stride * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                               numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                               cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+              "copying distances from GPU");
+    checkCuda(cudaMemcpy2DAsync(path.data(), numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                               deviceWitness, stride * sizeof(unsigned int),
+                               numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                               cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+              "copying paths from GPU");
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "waiting for Floyd-Warshall");
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroying CUDA stream");
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceDistance), "freeing GPU matrices");
 }

 #include <algorithm>
+#include <cerrno>
 #include <chrono>
 #include <cmath>
 #include <cstdio>
 #include <cstdlib>
 #include <cstring>
+#include <limits>
 #include <vector>
 
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+
 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
 
+namespace {
+
+constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int THREAD_ROWS = 8;
+constexpr int ROWS_PER_THREAD = TILE_SIZE / THREAD_ROWS;
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+struct Tile {
+    unsigned int distance[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    unsigned int witness[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+};
+
+// A witness is one plus the largest internal vertex on a shortest path; zero
+// denotes an unchanged direct edge. Among equal distances prefer the smallest
+// witness. This preserves the original ascending-k, strict-improvement path
+// matrix even though the blocked algorithm visits candidates in another order.
+__device__ __forceinline__ void relax(unsigned int& distance,
+                                      unsigned int& witness,
+                                      unsigned int leftDistance,
+                                      unsigned int rightDistance,
+                                      unsigned int leftWitness,
+                                      unsigned int rightWitness,
+                                      unsigned int intermediate) {
+    const unsigned int candidate = leftDistance + rightDistance;
+    if (candidate <= distance) {
+        const unsigned int candidateWitness =
+            max(max(leftWitness, rightWitness), intermediate);
+        if (candidate < distance || candidateWitness < witness) {
+            distance = candidate;
+            witness = candidateWitness;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ void loadTile(Tile& tile,
+                                         const unsigned int* distance,
+                                         const unsigned int* witness,
+                                         int tileRow, int tileColumn,
+                                         size_t stride) {
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        tile.distance[row][column] = distance[index];
+        tile.witness[row][column] = witness[index];
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ void storeTile(const Tile& tile,
+                                          unsigned int* distance,
+                                          unsigned int* witness,
+                                          int tileRow, int tileColumn,
+                                          size_t stride) {
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        distance[index] = tile.distance[row][column];
+        witness[index] = tile.witness[row][column];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void initializeDeviceMatrix(unsigned int* distance,
+                                        unsigned int* witness,
+                                        size_t stride, size_t numNodes) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        witness[index] = 0;
+        const size_t row = index / stride;
+        const size_t column = index % stride;
+        if (row >= numNodes || column >= numNodes) {
+            distance[index] = row == column ? 0 : INF;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Phase 1: close the diagonal tile. Pivot rows/columns are read-only during
+// each iteration (zero diagonal), so one barrier per k is sufficient.
+__global__ void closePivot(unsigned int* distance, unsigned int* witness,
+                           size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile tile;
+    loadTile(tile, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+    __syncthreads();
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            if (row != k && column != k) {
+                relax(tile.distance[row][column], tile.witness[row][column],
+                      tile.distance[row][k], tile.distance[k][column],
+                      tile.witness[row][k], tile.witness[k][column],
+                      pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    storeTile(tile, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+}
+
+// Phase 2: all tiles in the pivot row and column run independently.
+template <bool PivotRow>
+__global__ void closePanel(unsigned int* distance, unsigned int* witness,
+                           size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile diagonal;
+    __shared__ Tile panel;
+    const int other = int(blockIdx.x) + (int(blockIdx.x) >= pivot);
+    const int tileRow = PivotRow ? pivot : other;
+    const int tileColumn = PivotRow ? other : pivot;
+    loadTile(diagonal, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+    loadTile(panel, distance, witness, tileRow, tileColumn, stride);
+    __syncthreads();
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            if constexpr (PivotRow) {
+                if (row != k) {
+                    relax(panel.distance[row][column], panel.witness[row][column],
+                          diagonal.distance[row][k], panel.distance[k][column],
+                          diagonal.witness[row][k], panel.witness[k][column],
+                          pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+                }
+            } else {
+                if (column != k) {
+                    relax(panel.distance[row][column], panel.witness[row][column],
+                          panel.distance[row][k], diagonal.distance[k][column],
+                          panel.witness[row][k], diagonal.witness[k][column],
+                          pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    storeTile(panel, distance, witness, tileRow, tileColumn, stride);
+}
+
+// Phase 3 dominates the work: load each panel once, retain four output cells
+// per thread in registers, and reuse the panels without inner-loop barriers.
+__global__ void updateRemaining(unsigned int* __restrict__ distance,
+                                unsigned int* __restrict__ witness,
+                                size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile left;
+    __shared__ Tile right;
+    const int tileRow = int(blockIdx.y) + (int(blockIdx.y) >= pivot);
+    const int tileColumn = int(blockIdx.x) + (int(blockIdx.x) >= pivot);
+    loadTile(left, distance, witness, tileRow, pivot, stride);
+    loadTile(right, distance, witness, pivot, tileColumn, stride);
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    unsigned int outputDistance[ROWS_PER_THREAD];
+    unsigned int outputWitness[ROWS_PER_THREAD];
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        outputDistance[r] = distance[index];
+        outputWitness[r] = witness[index];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int rightDistance = right.distance[k][column];
+        const unsigned int rightWitness = right.witness[k][column];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            relax(outputDistance[r], outputWitness[r],
+                  left.distance[row][k], rightDistance,
+                  left.witness[row][k], rightWitness,
+                  pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        distance[index] = outputDistance[r];
+        witness[index] = outputWitness[r];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void decodePaths(unsigned int* witness, size_t stride) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const unsigned int value = witness[index];
+        // initializePathMatrix initializes each entry to its source row.
+        witness[index] = value ? value - 1 : unsigned(index / stride);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
 // Index calculation for flattened 2D array
 inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;
@@ -44,26 +256,62 @@

+namespace {
+
+constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int THREAD_ROWS = 8;
+constexpr int ROWS_PER_THREAD = TILE_SIZE / THREAD_ROWS;
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+struct Tile {
+    unsigned int distance[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    unsigned int witness[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+};
+
+// A witness is one plus the largest internal vertex on a shortest path; zero
+// denotes an unchanged direct edge. Among equal distances prefer the smallest
+// witness. This preserves the original ascending-k, strict-improvement path
+// matrix even though the blocked algorithm visits candidates in another order.
+__device__ __forceinline__ void relax(unsigned int& distance,
+                                      unsigned int& witness,
+                                      unsigned int leftDistance,
+                                      unsigned int rightDistance,
+                                      unsigned int leftWitness,
+                                      unsigned int rightWitness,
+                                      unsigned int intermediate) {
+    const unsigned int candidate = leftDistance + rightDistance;
+    if (candidate <= distance) {
+        const unsigned int candidateWitness =
+            max(max(leftWitness, rightWitness), intermediate);
+        if (candidate < distance || candidateWitness < witness) {
+            distance = candidate;
+            witness = candidateWitness;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ void loadTile(Tile& tile,
+                                         const unsigned int* distance,
+                                         const unsigned int* witness,
+                                         int tileRow, int tileColumn,
+                                         size_t stride) {
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        tile.distance[row][column] = distance[index];
+        tile.witness[row][column] = witness[index];
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ void storeTile(const Tile& tile,
+                                          unsigned int* distance,
+                                          unsigned int* witness,
+                                          int tileRow, int tileColumn,
+                                          size_t stride) {
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        distance[index] = tile.distance[row][column];
+        witness[index] = tile.witness[row][column];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void initializeDeviceMatrix(unsigned int* distance,
+                                        unsigned int* witness,
+                                        size_t stride, size_t numNodes) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        witness[index] = 0;
+        const size_t row = index / stride;
+        const size_t column = index % stride;
+        if (row >= numNodes || column >= numNodes) {
+            distance[index] = row == column ? 0 : INF;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Phase 1: close the diagonal tile. Pivot rows/columns are read-only during
+// each iteration (zero diagonal), so one barrier per k is sufficient.
+__global__ void closePivot(unsigned int* distance, unsigned int* witness,
+                           size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile tile;
+    loadTile(tile, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+    __syncthreads();
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            if (row != k && column != k) {
+                relax(tile.distance[row][column], tile.witness[row][column],
+                      tile.distance[row][k], tile.distance[k][column],
+                      tile.witness[row][k], tile.witness[k][column],
+                      pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    storeTile(tile, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+}
+
+// Phase 2: all tiles in the pivot row and column run independently.
+template <bool PivotRow>
+__global__ void closePanel(unsigned int* distance, unsigned int* witness,
+                           size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile diagonal;
+    __shared__ Tile panel;
+    const int other = int(blockIdx.x) + (int(blockIdx.x) >= pivot);
+    const int tileRow = PivotRow ? pivot : other;
+    const int tileColumn = PivotRow ? other : pivot;
+    loadTile(diagonal, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+    loadTile(panel, distance, witness, tileRow, tileColumn, stride);
+    __syncthreads();
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            if constexpr (PivotRow) {
+                if (row != k) {
+                    relax(panel.distance[row][column], panel.witness[row][column],
+                          diagonal.distance[row][k], panel.distance[k][column],
+                          diagonal.witness[row][k], panel.witness[k][column],
+                          pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+                }
+            } else {
+                if (column != k) {
+                    relax(panel.distance[row][column], panel.witness[row][column],
+                          panel.distance[row][k], diagonal.distance[k][column],
+                          panel.witness[row][k], diagonal.witness[k][column],
+                          pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    storeTile(panel, distance, witness, tileRow, tileColumn, stride);
+}
+
+// Phase 3 dominates the work: load each panel once, retain four output cells
+// per thread in registers, and reuse the panels without inner-loop barriers.
+__global__ void updateRemaining(unsigned int* __restrict__ distance,
+                                unsigned int* __restrict__ witness,
+                                size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile left;
+    __shared__ Tile right;
+    const int tileRow = int(blockIdx.y) + (int(blockIdx.y) >= pivot);
+    const int tileColumn = int(blockIdx.x) + (int(blockIdx.x) >= pivot);
+    loadTile(left, distance, witness, tileRow, pivot, stride);
+    loadTile(right, distance, witness, pivot, tileColumn, stride);
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    unsigned int outputDistance[ROWS_PER_THREAD];
+    unsigned int outputWitness[ROWS_PER_THREAD];
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        outputDistance[r] = distance[index];
+        outputWitness[r] = witness[index];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int rightDistance = right.distance[k][column];
+        const unsigned int rightWitness = right.witness[k][column];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            relax(outputDistance[r], outputWitness[r],
+                  left.distance[row][k], rightDistance,
+                  left.witness[row][k], rightWitness,
+                  pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        distance[index] = outputDistance[r];
+        witness[index] = outputWitness[r];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void decodePaths(unsigned int* witness, size_t stride) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const unsigned int value = witness[index];
+        // initializePathMatrix initializes each entry to its source row.
+        witness[index] = value ? value - 1 : unsigned(index / stride);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
 // Index calculation for flattened 2D array
 inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;

+    checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroying CUDA stream");

The CUDA implementation is ready to build. It processes diagonal, row/column, and remaining tiles in order, keeping the matrices on the GPU. I’ll check tile-boundary sizes against the CPU code and compare both distance and path entries.

+namespace {
+
+constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int THREAD_ROWS = 8;
+constexpr int ROWS_PER_THREAD = TILE_SIZE / THREAD_ROWS;

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}

+struct Tile {
+    unsigned int distance[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    unsigned int witness[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+};
+
+// A witness is one plus the largest internal vertex on a shortest path; zero
+// denotes an unchanged direct edge. Among equal distances prefer the smallest
+// witness. This preserves the original ascending-k, strict-improvement path
+// matrix even though the blocked algorithm visits candidates in another order.
+__device__ __forceinline__ void relax(unsigned int& distance,
+                                      unsigned int& witness,
+                                      unsigned int leftDistance,
+                                      unsigned int rightDistance,
+                                      unsigned int leftWitness,
+                                      unsigned int rightWitness,
+                                      unsigned int intermediate) {
+    const unsigned int candidate = leftDistance + rightDistance;
+    if (candidate <= distance) {
+        const unsigned int candidateWitness =
+            max(max(leftWitness, rightWitness), intermediate);
+        if (candidate < distance || candidateWitness < witness) {
+            distance = candidate;
+            witness = candidateWitness;
+        }
+    }
+}

+__device__ __forceinline__ void loadTile(Tile& tile,
+                                         const unsigned int* distance,
+                                         const unsigned int* witness,
+                                         int tileRow, int tileColumn,
+                                         size_t stride) {
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        tile.distance[row][column] = distance[index];
+        tile.witness[row][column] = witness[index];
+    }

+__global__ void initializeDeviceMatrix(unsigned int* distance,

+            distance[index] = row == column ? 0 : INF;
+        }

+// Phase 1: close the diagonal tile. Pivot rows/columns are read-only during
+// each iteration (zero diagonal), so one barrier per k is sufficient.
+__global__ void closePivot(unsigned int* distance, unsigned int* witness,
+                           size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile tile;
+    loadTile(tile, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+    __syncthreads();
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll

+            if (row != k && column != k) {

+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    storeTile(tile, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+}

+// Phase 2: all tiles in the pivot row and column run independently.
+template <bool PivotRow>
+__global__ void closePanel(unsigned int* distance, unsigned int* witness,
+                           size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile diagonal;
+    __shared__ Tile panel;

+    __syncthreads();
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {

+            if constexpr (PivotRow) {
+                if (row != k) {

+                          pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    storeTile(panel, distance, witness, tileRow, tileColumn, stride);
+}

+// Phase 3 dominates the work: load each panel once, retain four output cells
+// per thread in registers, and reuse the panels without inner-loop barriers.
+__global__ void updateRemaining(unsigned int* __restrict__ distance,
+                                unsigned int* __restrict__ witness,
+                                size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile left;
+    __shared__ Tile right;
+    const int tileRow = int(blockIdx.y) + (int(blockIdx.y) >= pivot);
+    const int tileColumn = int(blockIdx.x) + (int(blockIdx.x) >= pivot);
+    loadTile(left, distance, witness, tileRow, pivot, stride);
+    loadTile(right, distance, witness, pivot, tileColumn, stride);
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    unsigned int outputDistance[ROWS_PER_THREAD];
+    unsigned int outputWitness[ROWS_PER_THREAD];
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        outputDistance[r] = distance[index];
+        outputWitness[r] = witness[index];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int rightDistance = right.distance[k][column];
+        const unsigned int rightWitness = right.witness[k][column];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            relax(outputDistance[r], outputWitness[r],
+                  left.distance[row][k], rightDistance,
+                  left.witness[row][k], rightWitness,
+                  pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        distance[index] = outputDistance[r];
+        witness[index] = outputWitness[r];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void decodePaths(unsigned int* witness, size_t stride) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const unsigned int value = witness[index];
+        // initializePathMatrix initializes each entry to its source row.
+        witness[index] = value ? value - 1 : unsigned(index / stride);
+    }
+}

 #include <algorithm>
+#include <cerrno>
 #include <chrono>
 #include <cmath>
 #include <cstdio>
 #include <cstdlib>
 #include <cstring>
+#include <limits>
 #include <vector>
 
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+
 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
 
+namespace {
+
+constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int THREAD_ROWS = 8;
+constexpr int ROWS_PER_THREAD = TILE_SIZE / THREAD_ROWS;
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+struct Tile {
+    unsigned int distance[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    unsigned int witness[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+};
+
+// A witness is one plus the largest internal vertex on a shortest path; zero
+// denotes an unchanged direct edge. Among equal distances prefer the smallest
+// witness. This preserves the original ascending-k, strict-improvement path
+// matrix even though the blocked algorithm visits candidates in another order.
+__device__ __forceinline__ void relax(unsigned int& distance,
+                                      unsigned int& witness,
+                                      unsigned int leftDistance,
+                                      unsigned int rightDistance,
+                                      unsigned int leftWitness,
+                                      unsigned int rightWitness,
+                                      unsigned int intermediate) {
+    const unsigned int candidate = leftDistance + rightDistance;
+    if (candidate <= distance) {
+        const unsigned int candidateWitness =
+            max(max(leftWitness, rightWitness), intermediate);
+        if (candidate < distance || candidateWitness < witness) {
+            distance = candidate;
+            witness = candidateWitness;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ void loadTile(Tile& tile,
+                                         const unsigned int* distance,
+                                         const unsigned int* witness,
+                                         int tileRow, int tileColumn,
+                                         size_t stride) {
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        tile.distance[row][column] = distance[index];
+        tile.witness[row][column] = witness[index];
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ void storeTile(const Tile& tile,
+                                          unsigned int* distance,
+                                          unsigned int* witness,
+                                          int tileRow, int tileColumn,
+                                          size_t stride) {
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        distance[index] = tile.distance[row][column];
+        witness[index] = tile.witness[row][column];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void initializeDeviceMatrix(unsigned int* distance,
+                                        unsigned int* witness,
+                                        size_t stride, size_t numNodes) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        witness[index] = 0;
+        const size_t row = index / stride;
+        const size_t column = index % stride;
+        if (row >= numNodes || column >= numNodes) {
+            distance[index] = row == column ? 0 : INF;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Phase 1: close the diagonal tile. Pivot rows/columns are read-only during
+// each iteration (zero diagonal), so one barrier per k is sufficient.
+__global__ void closePivot(unsigned int* distance, unsigned int* witness,
+                           size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile tile;
+    loadTile(tile, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+    __syncthreads();
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            if (row != k && column != k) {
+                relax(tile.distance[row][column], tile.witness[row][column],
+                      tile.distance[row][k], tile.distance[k][column],
+                      tile.witness[row][k], tile.witness[k][column],
+                      pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    storeTile(tile, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+}
+
+// Phase 2: all tiles in the pivot row and column run independently.
+template <bool PivotRow>
+__global__ void closePanel(unsigned int* distance, unsigned int* witness,
+                           size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile diagonal;
+    __shared__ Tile panel;
+    const int other = int(blockIdx.x) + (int(blockIdx.x) >= pivot);
+    const int tileRow = PivotRow ? pivot : other;
+    const int tileColumn = PivotRow ? other : pivot;
+    loadTile(diagonal, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+    loadTile(panel, distance, witness, tileRow, tileColumn, stride);
+    __syncthreads();
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            if constexpr (PivotRow) {
+                if (row != k) {
+                    relax(panel.distance[row][column], panel.witness[row][column],
+                          diagonal.distance[row][k], panel.distance[k][column],
+                          diagonal.witness[row][k], panel.witness[k][column],
+                          pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+                }
+            } else {
+                if (column != k) {
+                    relax(panel.distance[row][column], panel.witness[row][column],
+                          panel.distance[row][k], diagonal.distance[k][column],
+                          panel.witness[row][k], diagonal.witness[k][column],
+                          pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    storeTile(panel, distance, witness, tileRow, tileColumn, stride);
+}
+
+// Phase 3 dominates the work: load each panel once, retain four output cells
+// per thread in registers, and reuse the panels without inner-loop barriers.
+__global__ void updateRemaining(unsigned int* __restrict__ distance,
+                                unsigned int* __restrict__ witness,
+                                size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile left;
+    __shared__ Tile right;
+    const int tileRow = int(blockIdx.y) + (int(blockIdx.y) >= pivot);
+    const int tileColumn = int(blockIdx.x) + (int(blockIdx.x) >= pivot);
+    loadTile(left, distance, witness, tileRow, pivot, stride);
+    loadTile(right, distance, witness, pivot, tileColumn, stride);
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    unsigned int outputDistance[ROWS_PER_THREAD];
+    unsigned int outputWitness[ROWS_PER_THREAD];
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        outputDistance[r] = distance[index];
+        outputWitness[r] = witness[index];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int rightDistance = right.distance[k][column];
+        const unsigned int rightWitness = right.witness[k][column];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            relax(outputDistance[r], outputWitness[r],
+                  left.distance[row][k], rightDistance,
+                  left.witness[row][k], rightWitness,
+                  pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        distance[index] = outputDistance[r];
+        witness[index] = outputWitness[r];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void decodePaths(unsigned int* witness, size_t stride) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const unsigned int value = witness[index];
+        // initializePathMatrix initializes each entry to its source row.
+        witness[index] = value ? value - 1 : unsigned(index / stride);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
 // Index calculation for flattened 2D array
 inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <random>
void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned>&,size_t,unsigned,unsigned);
void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned>&,size_t);
void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned>&,std::vector<unsigned>&,size_t);
void reference(std::vector<unsigned>& d,std::vector<unsigned>& p,size_t n) {
    for (size_t k=0;k<n;++k)
        for (size_t i=0;i<n;++i)
            for (size_t j=0;j<n;++j) {
                const unsigned v=d[i*n+k]+d[k*n+j];
                if(v<d[i*n+j]) { d[i*n+j]=v; p[i*n+j]=k; }
            }
}
int main() {
    const size_t sizes[]={0,1,2,7,15,31,32,33,63,64,65,95,96,97,127,128,129,255,256,257,511,512,513,777,1024};
    for(size_t n:sizes) {
        for(unsigned pattern=0;pattern<4;++pattern) {
            if(pattern && n>257) continue;
            std::vector<unsigned> d(n*n),p(n*n);
            initializeDistanceMatrix(d,n,1,200);
            initializePathMatrix(p,n);
            if(pattern) {
                std::mt19937 random(42+pattern);
                for(size_t i=0;i<n;++i)
                    for(size_t j=0;j<n;++j) {
                        unsigned v=pattern==1?1+random()%5:random()%17;
                        if(pattern==3 && random()%4"'!=0) v=1000000000;
                        d[i*n+j]=i==j?0:v;
                    }
            }
            auto expectedD=d,expectedP=p;
            reference(expectedD,expectedP,n);
            floydWarshall(d,p,n);
            for(size_t i=0;i<n*n;++i)
                if(d[i]!=expectedD[i] || p[i]!=expectedP[i]) {
                    std::printf("FAIL n=%zu pattern=%u at (%zu,%zu): distance %u/%u, path %u/%u'"\\n\",n,pattern,i/n,i%n,d[i],expectedD[i],p[i],expectedP[i]);
                    return 1;
                }
        }
        std::printf(\"PASS n=%zu: exact distance and path matrices\\n\",n);
    }
}

 #include <algorithm>
+#include <cerrno>
 #include <chrono>
 #include <cmath>
 #include <cstdio>
 #include <cstdlib>
 #include <cstring>
+#include <limits>
 #include <vector>
 
+#include <cuda_runtime.h>
+
 #include "../common/results_output.hpp"
 
 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
 
+namespace {
+
+constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 32;
+constexpr int THREAD_ROWS = 8;
+constexpr int ROWS_PER_THREAD = TILE_SIZE / THREAD_ROWS;
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation,
+                cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+struct Tile {
+    unsigned int distance[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    unsigned int witness[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+};
+
+// A witness is one plus the largest internal vertex on a shortest path; zero
+// denotes an unchanged direct edge. Among equal distances prefer the smallest
+// witness. This preserves the original ascending-k, strict-improvement path
+// matrix even though the blocked algorithm visits candidates in another order.
+__device__ __forceinline__ void relax(unsigned int& distance,
+                                      unsigned int& witness,
+                                      unsigned int leftDistance,
+                                      unsigned int rightDistance,
+                                      unsigned int leftWitness,
+                                      unsigned int rightWitness,
+                                      unsigned int intermediate) {
+    const unsigned int candidate = leftDistance + rightDistance;
+    if (candidate <= distance) {
+        const unsigned int candidateWitness =
+            max(max(leftWitness, rightWitness), intermediate);
+        if (candidate < distance || candidateWitness < witness) {
+            distance = candidate;
+            witness = candidateWitness;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ void loadTile(Tile& tile,
+                                         const unsigned int* distance,
+                                         const unsigned int* witness,
+                                         int tileRow, int tileColumn,
+                                         size_t stride) {
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        tile.distance[row][column] = distance[index];
+        tile.witness[row][column] = witness[index];
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ void storeTile(const Tile& tile,
+                                          unsigned int* distance,
+                                          unsigned int* witness,
+                                          int tileRow, int tileColumn,
+                                          size_t stride) {
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        distance[index] = tile.distance[row][column];
+        witness[index] = tile.witness[row][column];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void initializeDeviceMatrix(unsigned int* distance,
+                                        unsigned int* witness,
+                                        size_t stride, size_t numNodes) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        witness[index] = 0;
+        const size_t row = index / stride;
+        const size_t column = index % stride;
+        if (row >= numNodes || column >= numNodes) {
+            distance[index] = row == column ? 0 : INF;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Phase 1: close the diagonal tile. Pivot rows/columns are read-only during
+// each iteration (zero diagonal), so one barrier per k is sufficient.
+__global__ void closePivot(unsigned int* distance, unsigned int* witness,
+                           size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile tile;
+    loadTile(tile, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+    __syncthreads();
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            if (row != k && column != k) {
+                relax(tile.distance[row][column], tile.witness[row][column],
+                      tile.distance[row][k], tile.distance[k][column],
+                      tile.witness[row][k], tile.witness[k][column],
+                      pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    storeTile(tile, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+}
+
+// Phase 2: all tiles in the pivot row and column run independently.
+template <bool PivotRow>
+__global__ void closePanel(unsigned int* distance, unsigned int* witness,
+                           size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile diagonal;
+    __shared__ Tile panel;
+    const int other = int(blockIdx.x) + (int(blockIdx.x) >= pivot);
+    const int tileRow = PivotRow ? pivot : other;
+    const int tileColumn = PivotRow ? other : pivot;
+    loadTile(diagonal, distance, witness, pivot, pivot, stride);
+    loadTile(panel, distance, witness, tileRow, tileColumn, stride);
+    __syncthreads();
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            if constexpr (PivotRow) {
+                if (row != k) {
+                    relax(panel.distance[row][column], panel.witness[row][column],
+                          diagonal.distance[row][k], panel.distance[k][column],
+                          diagonal.witness[row][k], panel.witness[k][column],
+                          pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+                }
+            } else {
+                if (column != k) {
+                    relax(panel.distance[row][column], panel.witness[row][column],
+                          panel.distance[row][k], diagonal.distance[k][column],
+                          panel.witness[row][k], diagonal.witness[k][column],
+                          pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    storeTile(panel, distance, witness, tileRow, tileColumn, stride);
+}
+
+// Phase 3 dominates the work: load each panel once, retain four output cells
+// per thread in registers, and reuse the panels without inner-loop barriers.
+__global__ void updateRemaining(unsigned int* __restrict__ distance,
+                                unsigned int* __restrict__ witness,
+                                size_t stride, int pivot) {
+    __shared__ Tile left;
+    __shared__ Tile right;
+    const int tileRow = int(blockIdx.y) + (int(blockIdx.y) >= pivot);
+    const int tileColumn = int(blockIdx.x) + (int(blockIdx.x) >= pivot);
+    loadTile(left, distance, witness, tileRow, pivot, stride);
+    loadTile(right, distance, witness, pivot, tileColumn, stride);
+    const int column = threadIdx.x;
+    unsigned int outputDistance[ROWS_PER_THREAD];
+    unsigned int outputWitness[ROWS_PER_THREAD];
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        outputDistance[r] = distance[index];
+        outputWitness[r] = witness[index];
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int rightDistance = right.distance[k][column];
+        const unsigned int rightWitness = right.witness[k][column];
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+            const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+            relax(outputDistance[r], outputWitness[r],
+                  left.distance[row][k], rightDistance,
+                  left.witness[row][k], rightWitness,
+                  pivot * TILE_SIZE + k + 1);
+        }
+    }
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int r = 0; r < ROWS_PER_THREAD; ++r) {
+        const int row = threadIdx.y + r * THREAD_ROWS;
+        const size_t index = (size_t(tileRow) * TILE_SIZE + row) * stride +
+                            size_t(tileColumn) * TILE_SIZE + column;
+        distance[index] = outputDistance[r];
+        witness[index] = outputWitness[r];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void decodePaths(unsigned int* witness, size_t stride) {
+    const size_t count = stride * stride;
+    for (size_t index = size_t(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         index < count; index += size_t(gridDim.x) * blockDim.x) {
+        const unsigned int value = witness[index];
+        // initializePathMatrix initializes each entry to its source row.
+        witness[index] = value ? value - 1 : unsigned(index / stride);
+    }
+}
+
+} // namespace
+
 // Index calculation for flattened 2D array
 inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {
     return j * n + i;
@@ -44,26 +256,62 @@

+                               numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int), numNodes,
+                               cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+              "copying paths from GPU");
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "waiting for Floyd-Warshall");
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamDestroy(stream), "destroying CUDA stream");
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceDistance), "freeing GPU matrices");
 }
